That’s the main thing.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like people outside of work, like neighbors or friends, are watching you or trying to interfere with your life in some way?\n\nPatient: Not really neighbors or friends… I don’t talk to people like that much. They keep acting like I’m crazy, but I know they’re watching what I do and messing with the pallet counts so I don’t get that supervisor spot.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like someone is putting thoughts into your mind or controlling your actions against your will?\n\nPatient: Not like… thoughts put in my head, no. It’s more that my husband and the people at the warehouse are watching what I do and messing with the pallet counts, trying to keep me from getting that supervisor spot.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt that your thoughts are not your own, or that someone is placing thoughts in your mind that you didn’t think yourself?\n\nPatient: No… not like somebody putting thoughts in my head.
